# 08 — Transcriptions of handwritten sources: Dora Ella Newnam Stone

Conventions: `[?]` uncertain · `[illegible]` unreadable · `W[illia]m` expansion · `{note}` editorial only.

## DOC001 — 1880 U.S. census, Lee County, Kentucky, ED 59, sheet 6
- Image file(s) + SHA-256: `sources/census/1880-lee-ky-ed59-sheet6-p730-elias-dora.jpg` · `ce48bc3c6a4021976aa480def6b1cc080aced108005d2861f8c144396039b36f`
- Page: IA `10thcensus0427unit` n730; NARA T9 reel 427; enumerator page 6, SD 3, ED 59
- Provenance: NARA microfilm via Internet Archive, accessed 27 Oct 2026. Saw: scan (JPEG of microfilm). Original paper not seen ☐
- Language / script / hands: English; one enumerator hand (James J. Hampton), American cursive 1880 census bookhand
- Image quality: **sufficient** for names/ages/relationships; parent-birthplace columns and some given names of *neighbors* have `[?]` at this resolution. Full-page including left margin. No re-capture strictly required for Dora’s line; a higher-resolution JP2 from the IA zip would help neighbor names.
- Enhancements: none (viewed as downloaded `_w1000.jpg`)

### Diplomatic transcription (household 46/46 only; Pass 1+2 from the JPEG)

Header (normalised layout):
> Page No. 6 · Supervisor’s Dist. No. 3 · Enumeration Dist. No. 59  
> Schedule 1. — Inhabitants in District No 59, in the County of Lee, State of Kentucky  
> enumerated by me on the 5[?] day of June, 1880. / James J. Hampton, Enumerator.

Dwelling 46, family 46 (lines as counted on the sheet; Soundex “line 11” matches the head):

| Line (approx.) | Name | Color | Sex | Age | Relation | Occupation | Birth | Father b. | Mother b. |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 11 | Newnam, Elias | W | M | 38 | {head} | Farmer | Kentucky | {KY — ticks; not re-read digitwise} | {KY} |
| 12 | — Mary | W | F | 38 | Wife | Keeping House | Kentucky | | |
| 13 | — William | W | M | 21 | Son | Works on farm | Kentucky | | |
| 14 | — Malissa | W | F | 16 | Daughter | At home | Kentucky | | |
| 15 | — Francis | W | F | 14 | Daughter | At home | Kentucky | | |
| 16 | — Mary | W | F | 10 | Daughter | {At home / blank} | Kentucky | | |
| 17 | — **Dora** | W | F | **8** | Daughter | | Kentucky | | |
| 18 | — Catharine | W | F | 6 | Daughter | | Kentucky | | |

Pass 2 notes: Surname on the page is **Newnam** (not Newman) — same spelling as the Soundex card. Given name **Dora** is clear (D-o-r-a). Age **8** is a single digit, not 3 or 5. Sex F, relation Daughter. Next household 47/47 is also Newnam (Joseph, 38). Household 48/48 John Newnam, 60. Those neighbor lines were **not** fully transcribed this pass (crowded hand).

### Normalised reading
Elias Newnam, 38, farmer, and wife Mary, 38, with children William 21, Malissa 16, Francis 14, Mary 10, Dora 8, Catharine 6, all born Kentucky, Lee County ED 59, 5 June 1880.

### Facts extracted → claim IDs
- “Dora” “8” “Daughter” “Kentucky” → C003, C001 (year 1871)
- Head “Elias” “Newnam” → C004
- Catharine 6 → C044

## DOC007 — 1870 U.S. census, Lee County, Kentucky, Sturgeon Precinct, p. 4 (337b)

### Diplomatic transcription (household 19/19 only; Pass 1+2)

Header:
> Page No. 4 · Inhabitants in Sturgeon Precinct, County of Lee, State of Kentucky  
> enumerated by me on the 29 day of June, 1870. / Chas. D. Tyler, Ass’t Marshal.

| Line | Dw/Fam | Name | Age | Sex | Color | Occupation | Birth |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 6 | 19/19 | Newman, John | 50 | M | W | Farm labourer | Kentucky |
| 7 | | — Elizabeth | 25 | F | W | Keeping house | Kentucky |
| 8 | | — Mary | 21 | F | W | | Kentucky |
| 9 | | — Sarah | 18 | F | W | | Kentucky |
| 10 | | — Celia | 16 | F | W | | Kentucky |
| 11 | | — Margret | 9 | F | W | | Kentucky |
| 12 | | — Nancy | 8 | F | W | | Kentucky |
| 13 | | — John | 7 | M | W | | Kentucky |
| 14 | | — Sousanna | 5 | F | W | | Kentucky |
| 15 | | — **Samuel** | **1** | M | W | | Kentucky |

Pass 2: Surname on this page is **Newman** (one “n”), vs **Newnam** on the 1880 schedule — clerk variant, same Soundex. **Samuel** age **1** is a single digit. Elizabeth 25 cannot be the biological mother of Mary 21 on any ordinary reading; the older girls are a prior wife’s or John’s siblings — relationship column in 1870 does not state kinship beyond implied household. USGenNet 337b (S043) agrees on the ten names and ages.

### Facts extracted → claim IDs
- Samuel, 1, in John & Elizabeth’s Sturgeon house, 29 Jun 1870 → C062
- Conflict #9 (not merged with 1880 dw 3)

Jan 1892 8; Rosey d. May 1894 6. Continuation 1679: Jesse C. son b. Mar 1896 age 4. **Closest age to 1920 Tom A. 62. Not merged** (no middle A.; wife Nancy).

Mch 1876 24 **Ohio**; Gertrude M. d. Nov 1893 6 Missouri; **Jenkins, Ivory** G M Jany 1825 75 TN. **The card does not write Dunklin County.** Volume 33 = Greene Co. Soundex (S094). Dunklin village nil (C105). **Original schedule read DOC018 (C117).** Not merged with 1920 Tom A. (age gap).

5. PDF 1727 — **Stone, Tom.** B, May 1878, 21, Kentucky. Mississippi Co., Tywappity Twp. Laborer with Drinkwater, Fred. **Race Black — rejected.**

None is Dora Ella Newnam of Sturgeon, ~39.

| 38 | | — **Minerva** | **48** | **F** | W | Keeping House |
| 39 | | — **Amelia** | **21** | F | W | |
| 40 | | — **Elihu** | **19** | M | W | at Home |

| Line | Name | Age | Sex | Occupation |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | — **John** | **18** | M | at Home |
| 2 | — **Margret** | **16** | F | |
| 3 | — **Levi** | **14** | M | at Home |

Pass 2: Head is **Silas**, not Silus. Minerva is **F**. **No William** in this house (the page ends the household at Levi 14). Jackson Brandenburg at dw 12 is a neighbour, not merged with James 62 of Proctor.

### Normalised reading
Silas Gray, 54, farmer, and Minerva, 48, with Amelia 21, Elihu 19, John 18, Margret 16, and Levi 14, Sturgeon Precinct, 28–30 June 1870. William Gray, 23, was enumerated the same week in James Brandenburg’s Proctor house, not here.

- Kendrick Silus / sex M withdrawn → C357 C358

**PDF 433** {stamp **321A**, last Owsley} — Gipson continuation: **Sarah Gipson** 3 F, **Woodson** 1 M. First head: **Joseph Thomas** 51 M, dw **592/592**. Last head: **Benjamin McGuire** 45 M, dw **593/593**. Then blank lines, printed **Male / Female / Total**, page foot **14 m.** Did **not** read past this page.
- Examined, not hits: **Elisha** 12 M, **Eli** 10 M, **Levi** 11 M, **Elijah** 8 M (Thomas house); McGuire last child **Elyza**[?] 6 M (y-descender after l; alt Eliza; **not** Silas/Sylus).
- **NIL Silas. NIL Gray.**

### Pass 2
Re-read after line-crops, contrast copies, and an independent 240-dpi household crop of dw **581**. **Sylvania** 9 F stands. **Cyrus** 24 M is **WITHDRAWN**; the ink is **Squire** 24 M. **Ezekiel** Callahan is not Cyrus. **Jesse** lines are J-e-ss-e. No **Gray/Grey** surname on these ten clips (Gipson, Gunn, Sparks ≠ Gray).
